codex
The existing code is an unblocked, fully replicated implementation. I’m switching it to cyclic row ownership: every rank stores and computes only its rows, the owner of each pivot broadcasts the completed pivot row, and later rows update concurrently. This balances the shrinking Cholesky workload across ranks and avoids replicating the factor matrix. Matrix generation and validation will also be distributed so setup/checking do not become root-only memory bottlenecks.

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;

+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
     }

-    return true;

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    return global_relative <= 1e-6;
 }
 
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    return global_relative <= 1e-6;
 }
 
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");

+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    return global_relative <= 1e-6;
 }
 
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");

+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    return global_relative <= 1e-6;
 }
 
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}
+
+    double* row(size_t local_row) { return data.data() + local_row * n; }
+    const double* row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return data.data() + local_row * n;
+    }
+    size_t global_row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + local_row * static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    }
+};
+
+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the exact same pseudo-random B as the original benchmark, retaining
+// only locally owned rows.  A = B B^T is then formed cooperatively.  Only A's
+// lower triangle is needed by both factorization and validation.
+static void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                           MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> local_b(A.local_rows * A.n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < A.n; ++i) {
+        const bool owned = static_cast<int>(i % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) == A.rank;
+        double* destination = owned ? local_b.data() + (i / A.ranks) * A.n : nullptr;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) {
+            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+            if (owned) destination[k] = value;
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> b_row(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            std::copy_n(local_b.data() + (j / A.ranks) * A.n, A.n, b_row.data());
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(b_row.data(), A.n, owner, comm);

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}
+
+    double* row(size_t local_row) { return data.data() + local_row * n; }
+    const double* row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return data.data() + local_row * n;
+    }
+    size_t global_row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + local_row * static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    }
+};
+
+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the exact same pseudo-random B as the original benchmark, retaining
+// only locally owned rows.  A = B B^T is then formed cooperatively.  Only A's
+// lower triangle is needed by both factorization and validation.
+static void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                           MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> local_b(A.local_rows * A.n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < A.n; ++i) {
+        const bool owned = static_cast<int>(i % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) == A.rank;
+        double* destination = owned ? local_b.data() + (i / A.ranks) * A.n : nullptr;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) {
+            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+            if (owned) destination[k] = value;
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> b_row(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            std::copy_n(local_b.data() + (j / A.ranks) * A.n, A.n, b_row.data());
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(b_row.data(), A.n, owner, comm);

+static bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& A, size_t& failed_diagonal,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        int positive = 1;
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            double* const diagonal_row = A.row(j / A.ranks);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
+                sum += diagonal_row[k] * diagonal_row[k];
+            const double value = diagonal_row[j] - sum;
+            positive = value > 0.0;
+            if (positive) {

+                std::copy_n(diagonal_row, j + 1, pivot.data());

+        if (!positive) {

+            return false;
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);

+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(A.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(A.rank)) / A.ranks + 1;

+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            double* const current = A.row(lr);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += current[k] * pivot[k];
+            current[j] = (current[j] - sum) * inverse_diagonal;

     }

+        std::fill(A.row(lr) + i + 1, A.row(lr) + A.n, 0.0);

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}
+
+    double* row(size_t local_row) { return data.data() + local_row * n; }
+    const double* row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return data.data() + local_row * n;
+    }
+    size_t global_row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + local_row * static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    }
+};
+
+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the exact same pseudo-random B as the original benchmark, retaining
+// only locally owned rows.  A = B B^T is then formed cooperatively.  Only A's
+// lower triangle is needed by both factorization and validation.
+static void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                           MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> local_b(A.local_rows * A.n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < A.n; ++i) {
+        const bool owned = static_cast<int>(i % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) == A.rank;
+        double* destination = owned ? local_b.data() + (i / A.ranks) * A.n : nullptr;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) {
+            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+            if (owned) destination[k] = value;
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> b_row(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            std::copy_n(local_b.data() + (j / A.ranks) * A.n, A.n, b_row.data());
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(b_row.data(), A.n, owner, comm);

+static bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& A, size_t& failed_diagonal,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        int positive = 1;
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            double* const diagonal_row = A.row(j / A.ranks);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
+                sum += diagonal_row[k] * diagonal_row[k];
+            const double value = diagonal_row[j] - sum;
+            positive = value > 0.0;
+            if (positive) {
+                diagonal_row[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                std::copy_n(diagonal_row, j + 1, pivot.data());
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&positive, 1, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        if (!positive) {
+            failed_diagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(A.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(A.rank)) / A.ranks + 1;
+        const double inverse_diagonal = 1.0 / pivot[j];
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            double* const current = A.row(lr);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += current[k] * pivot[k];
+            current[j] = (current[j] - sum) * inverse_diagonal;
         }
     }
-

+    // Preserve the original program's full row-major lower-triangular output.

+        const size_t i = A.global_row(lr);
+        std::fill(A.row(lr) + i + 1, A.row(lr) + A.n, 0.0);
+    }
     return true;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}
+
+    double* row(size_t local_row) { return data.data() + local_row * n; }
+    const double* row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return data.data() + local_row * n;
+    }
+    size_t global_row(size_t local_row) const {
+        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + local_row * static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    }
+};
+
+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the exact same pseudo-random B as the original benchmark, retaining
+// only locally owned rows.  A = B B^T is then formed cooperatively.  Only A's
+// lower triangle is needed by both factorization and validation.
+static void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                           MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> local_b(A.local_rows * A.n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < A.n; ++i) {
+        const bool owned = static_cast<int>(i % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) == A.rank;
+        double* destination = owned ? local_b.data() + (i / A.ranks) * A.n : nullptr;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) {
+            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+            if (owned) destination[k] = value;
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> b_row(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            std::copy_n(local_b.data() + (j / A.ranks) * A.n, A.n, b_row.data());
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(b_row.data(), A.n, owner, comm);

+static bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& A, size_t& failed_diagonal,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(A.n);
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
+        int positive = 1;
+        if (A.rank == owner) {
+            double* const diagonal_row = A.row(j / A.ranks);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
+                sum += diagonal_row[k] * diagonal_row[k];
+            const double value = diagonal_row[j] - sum;
+            positive = value > 0.0;
+            if (positive) {
+                diagonal_row[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                std::copy_n(diagonal_row, j + 1, pivot.data());
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&positive, 1, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        if (!positive) {
+            failed_diagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(A.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(A.rank)) / A.ranks + 1;
+        const double inverse_diagonal = 1.0 / pivot[j];
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            double* const current = A.row(lr);
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += current[k] * pivot[k];
+            current[j] = (current[j] - sum) * inverse_diagonal;
         }
     }
-

+    // Preserve the original program's full row-major lower-triangular output.

+        const size_t i = A.global_row(lr);
+        std::fill(A.row(lr) + i + 1, A.row(lr) + A.n, 0.0);
+    }

+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());

+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);

+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {

+    return valid != 0;

The distributed runs now produce identical result hashes at 1 and 4 ranks, including the edge case where there are more ranks than matrix rows. I’m doing one final semantics pass now: retaining the original per-element floating-point operation order where possible, while keeping the MPI distribution and balanced work scheduling.

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}

+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+
+static void printUsage(const char* program) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
+// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
+          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
+                         : 0),
+          data(local_rows * n_) {}

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
+        values += chunk;
+        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");

+// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n;
+    int rank;
+    int ranks;
+    size_t local_rows;

+    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
+        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),

+                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
+// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    while (count != 0) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");

+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

    14	// Rows are distributed cyclically.  Unlike a contiguous distribution, this
    15	// keeps all ranks busy as the active lower-right part of the matrix shrinks.
    16	struct DistributedMatrix {
    17	    size_t n;
    18	    int rank;
    19	    int ranks;
    20	    size_t local_rows;
    21	    std::vector<double> data;
    22	
    23	    DistributedMatrix(size_t n_, int rank_, int ranks_)
    24	        : n(n_), rank(rank_), ranks(ranks_),
    25	          local_rows(static_cast<size_t>(rank_) < n_
    26	                         ? 1 + (n_ - 1 - static_cast<size_t>(rank_)) /
    27	                                   static_cast<size_t>(ranks_)
    28	                         : 0),
    29	          data(local_rows * n_) {}
    30	
    31	    double* row(size_t local_row) { return data.data() + local_row * n; }
    32	    const double* row(size_t local_row) const {
    33	        return data.data() + local_row * n;
    34	    }
    35	    size_t global_row(size_t local_row) const {
    36	        return static_cast<size_t>(rank) + local_row * static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
    37	    }
    38	};
    39	
    40	// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for
    41	// matrices whose rows are larger than INT_MAX elements.
    42	static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,
    43	                             MPI_Comm comm) {
    44	    while (count != 0) {
    45	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(count,
    46	                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
    47	        MPI_Bcast(values, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, comm);
    48	        values += chunk;
    49	        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
    50	    }
    51	}
    52	
    53	// Generate the exact same pseudo-random B as the original benchmark, retaining
    54	// only locally owned rows.  A = B B^T is then formed cooperatively.  Only A's
    55	// lower triangle is needed by both factorization and validation.
    56	static void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& A,
    57	                                           MPI_Comm comm) {
    58	    std::vector<double> local_b(A.local_rows * A.n);
    59	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    60	    for (size_t i = 0; i < A.n; ++i) {
    61	        const bool owned = static_cast<int>(i % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) == A.rank;
    62	        double* destination = owned ? local_b.data() + (i / A.ranks) * A.n : nullptr;
    63	        for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) {
    64	            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
    65	            if (owned) destination[k] = value;
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    std::vector<double> b_row(A.n);
    70	    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
    71	        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
    72	        if (A.rank == owner) {
    73	            std::copy_n(local_b.data() + (j / A.ranks) * A.n, A.n, b_row.data());
    74	        }
    75	        broadcastDoubles(b_row.data(), A.n, owner, comm);
    76	
    77	        size_t first = 0;
    78	        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(A.rank))
    79	            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(A.rank) + A.ranks - 1) / A.ranks;
    80	        for (size_t lr = first; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
    81	            const double* const own_b = local_b.data() + lr * A.n;
    82	            double sum = 0.0;
    83	            for (size_t k = 0; k < A.n; ++k) sum += own_b[k] * b_row[k];
    84	            const size_t i = A.global_row(lr);
    85	            A.row(lr)[j] = sum + (i == j ? static_cast<double>(A.n) : 0.0);
    86	        }
    87	    }
    88	}
    89	
    90	static bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& A, size_t& failed_diagonal,
    91	                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
    92	    std::vector<double> pivot(A.n);
    93	    for (size_t j = 0; j < A.n; ++j) {
    94	        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks));
    95	        int positive = 1;
    96	        if (A.rank == owner) {
    97	            double* const diagonal_row = A.row(j / A.ranks);
    98	            double sum = 0.0;
    99	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
   100	                sum += diagonal_row[k] * diagonal_row[k];
   101	            const double value = diagonal_row[j] - sum;
   102	            positive = value > 0.0;
   103	            if (positive) {
   104	                diagonal_row[j] = std::sqrt(value);
   105	                std::copy_n(diagonal_row, j + 1, pivot.data());
   106	            }
   107	        }
   108	        MPI_Bcast(&positive, 1, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
   109	        if (!positive) {
   110	            failed_diagonal = j;
   111	            return false;
   112	        }
   113	        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
   114	
   115	        size_t first = 0;
   116	        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(A.rank))
   117	            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(A.rank)) / A.ranks + 1;
   118	        for (size_t lr = first; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
   119	            double* const current = A.row(lr);
   120	            double sum = 0.0;
   121	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += current[k] * pivot[k];
   122	            current[j] = (current[j] - sum) / pivot[j];
   123	        }
   124	    }
   125	
   126	    // Preserve the original program's full row-major lower-triangular output.
   127	    for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
   128	        const size_t i = A.global_row(lr);
   129	        std::fill(A.row(lr) + i + 1, A.row(lr) + A.n, 0.0);
   130	    }
   131	    return true;
   132	}
   133	
   134	static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
   135	                             const std::vector<double>& original,
   136	                             MPI_Comm comm) {
   137	    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
   138	    double local_absolute = 0.0;
   139	    double local_relative = 0.0;
   140	
   141	    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
   142	        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
   143	        if (L.rank == owner)
   144	            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
   145	        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
   146	
   147	        size_t first = 0;
   148	        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
   149	            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
   150	        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
   151	            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
   152	            double reconstructed = 0.0;
   153	            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
   154	            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
   155	            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
   156	            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
   157	            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
   158	                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    double global_absolute = 0.0;
   163	    double global_relative = 0.0;
   164	    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
   165	    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
   166	    if (L.rank == 0) {
   167	        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
   168	        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
   169	    }
   170	    int valid = 1;
   171	    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
   172	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
   173	    return valid != 0;
   174	}
   175	
   176	static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
   177	                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
   178	    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
   179	    std::vector<double> result;
   180	    if (A.rank == 0) {
   181	        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
   182	        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
   183	            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
   184	                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
   185	        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
   186	            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
   187	                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
   188	                size_t remaining = A.n;
   189	                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
   190	                while (remaining != 0) {
   191	                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
   192	                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
   193	                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
   194	                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   195	                    destination += chunk;
   196	                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
   197	                }
   198	            }
   199	        }
   200	    } else {
   201	        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
   202	            size_t remaining = A.n;
   203	            const double* source = A.row(lr);
   204	            while (remaining != 0) {
   205	                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
   206	                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
   207	                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
   208	                source += chunk;
   209	                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
   210	            }
   211	        }
   212	    }
   213	    return result;
   214	}

+        std::fill(A.row(lr) + i + 1, A.row(lr) + A.n, 0.0);
     }
-    
     return true;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+static bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& L,
+                             const std::vector<double>& original,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    std::vector<double> pivot(L.n);
+    double local_absolute = 0.0;
+    double local_relative = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < L.n; ++j) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(j % static_cast<size_t>(L.ranks));
+        if (L.rank == owner)
+            std::copy_n(L.row(j / L.ranks), j + 1, pivot.data());
+        broadcastDoubles(pivot.data(), j + 1, owner, comm);
+
+        size_t first = 0;
+        if (j >= static_cast<size_t>(L.rank))
+            first = (j - static_cast<size_t>(L.rank) + L.ranks - 1) / L.ranks;
+        for (size_t lr = first; lr < L.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            const double* const row = L.row(lr);
+            double reconstructed = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) reconstructed += row[k] * pivot[k];
+            const double expected = original[lr * L.n + j];
+            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - expected);
+            local_absolute = std::max(local_absolute, error);
+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cholesky` executable name.

Key changes:

- Cyclic row distribution for balanced factorization work.
- Pivot-row broadcasts using MPI collectives.
- Distributed matrix generation, storage, and validation.
- Root-only reporting and optional result gathering.
- Peak-rank timing via `MPI_Wtime`.
- MPI-required CMake configuration.

Files updated:

- [cholesky.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cholesky_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r2/cholesky/cholesky.cpp:14)

Verification completed:

- Clean CMake configuration and warning-free build.
- Validation passed for 1, 2, and 4 MPI ranks.
- Edge case with more ranks than matrix rows passed.
- 1-rank and 4-rank result hashes matched exactly.

+// MPI counts are ints.  Chunking also makes all bulk operations valid for

+static void broadcastDoubles(double* values, size_t count, int root,

+            local_relative = std::max(local_relative,
+                                      error / (std::fabs(expected) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    double global_absolute = 0.0;
+    double global_relative = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_absolute, &global_absolute, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_relative, &global_relative, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (L.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", global_absolute);
+        std::printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", global_relative);
+    }
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (L.rank == 0) valid = global_relative <= 1e-6;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+
+static std::vector<double> gatherMatrix(const DistributedMatrix& A,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    constexpr int row_tag = 17;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (A.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(A.n * A.n);
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr)
+            std::copy_n(A.row(lr), A.n,
+                        result.data() + A.global_row(lr) * A.n);
+        for (int source = 1; source < A.ranks; ++source) {
+            for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(source); i < A.n;
+                 i += static_cast<size_t>(A.ranks)) {
+                size_t remaining = A.n;
+                double* destination = result.data() + i * A.n;
+                while (remaining != 0) {
+                    const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                        remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                    MPI_Recv(destination, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, row_tag,
+                             comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                    destination += chunk;
+                    remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t lr = 0; lr < A.local_rows; ++lr) {
+            size_t remaining = A.n;
+            const double* source = A.row(lr);
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+                    remaining, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+                MPI_Send(source, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, row_tag, comm);
+                source += chunk;
+                remaining -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cholesky` executable name.

Key changes:

- Cyclic row distribution for balanced factorization work.
- Pivot-row broadcasts using MPI collectives.
- Distributed matrix generation, storage, and validation.
- Root-only reporting and optional result gathering.
- Peak-rank timing via `MPI_Wtime`.
- MPI-required CMake configuration.
